every pool = no filter, the ideal trade on every liquid pool in the band; kept = the rug-vs-win kept half; vs all = kept mean - every-pool mean; gap = kept - skipped (ideal); rand< = share of random keeps of the same size that did worse than kept; sz / rw50 = real trades, size-weighted (P&L / money traded)
